[\[98a.2\]]btab pas/_'chi ba'i dus la bab cing mig shor zhing lce yang rdog por song ba'o/_/tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo zhes bya ba ni byams pa dang snying rje che zhing thos pa che ba/_sems can thams cad la snying rje mdzad pa/_sangs rgyas kyi bka' las mi 'da' ba/_'dul ba'i sgo nas 
[\[98a.3\]]dgra bcom pa rnams la bsnyen bkur mdzad pa/_sdom pa la gnas shing /_snying rje dang ldan pa kun dga' bos ni/_dge slong sa ri nad kyis btab pa mthong ngo /_/mthong bas rings pas bcom ldan 'das ga la der song ste zhes bya ba ni/_sbrul nag pos zin pa mthong ste/_
[\[98a.4\]]thabs mi rnyed cing thabs kyi don la bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar phyin no/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te zhes bya ba ni/_dge slong sa ri snying rje zhing bcom ldan 'das la gus pas phyag 'tshal te/_lan gsum bskor ba byas nas bcom ldan 
[\[98a.5\]]'das la'o/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug nas zhes bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nyan thos dang /_dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_dge bsnyen dang /_dge bsnyen ma dang /_lha dang mi la sogs pa'i 
[\[98a.6\]]dbus su'o/_/bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bos 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba ni/_dge slong sa ri'i don du bcom ldan 'das la zhu ba phul ba'o/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba ni/_rang bzhin gyis btsun pa ste/_de yang sku gsung 
[\[98a.7\]]thugs thul zhing /_mi dge ba bcu spangs shing dge ba bcu dang du len pa ste/_sems can gyi don du rang bzhin gyis rab tu byung ba'o/_/de bzhin du stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can la snying rjes khyab pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ni gti mug 
[\[98b\]]
[\[98b.1\]]dang /_zhe sdang dang /_'dod chags dang /_ser sna dang /_phrag dog dang /_de rnams bcom zhing 'khor ba las log pa'o/_/yang na bag chags kyi shes bya'i sgrib pa bcom pa'o/_/ldan pa ni skal ba dang ldan pa'o/_/'das pa ni 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa'o/_/mnyan 
[\[98b.2\]]yod na zhes bya ba ni/_yul ma ga d+hA'o/_/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba 'di na zhes bya ba ni/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nyan thos dgra bcom pa dang /_dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_dge bsnyen dang dge bsnyen ma 
[\[98b.3\]]la sogs pa'i gnas/_yang na dngos grub kyis gces pa'o/_/dge slong sa ri zhes bya ba ni bram ze'i rigs las bos pa ste/_dge slong yang rab tu byung ba'i ming grags pa'o/_/de gnyis 'dzom pas na dge slong sa ri zhes pa'o/_/gzhon nu thor bu lang tsho la bab pa zhes bya ba ni/_lo 
[\[98b.4\]]bcu gnyis lon pa ste/_gzhon nu'i dus su rab tu byung zhing 'dul ba ni mi shes pa'o/_/rab tu byung nas ring po ma lon pa zhes bya ba ni/_rab tu byung nas zhag bdun lon pa/_dge slong gi spyod pa mi shes pa/_rtse ba la dga' ba'o/_/bsnyen par rdzogs nas ring po ma lon pa 
[\[98b.5\]]zhes bya ba ni/_bsnyen par rdzogs nas zhag gcig lon pa'o/_/chos 'dul ba 'dir mchis nas ring po ma lon pa zhig gnas pa de zhes bya ba ni/_lus ngag yid gsum 'dul bas na 'dul ba ste/_chos kyang de nas gnas pa'o/_/'dir mchis nas ring po ma lon pa dge 
[\[98b.6\]]slong gi spyod pa mi shes shing /_gzhon nu'i spyod pa la gnas pa'o/_/dge 'dun gyi bsro khang bgyi ba'i slad du shing gshegs pa dang /_shing rul pa'i ser ka zhig nas sbrul nag po chen po zhig byung nas zhes bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das dge slong sa ri rtsed mo la phyin pa ste/_klu 
[\[98b.7\]]gdol ba'i rigs mche ba'i dug can 'jigs su rung ba dug rnam pa bzhi'i rang bzhin can/_shing rul pa gshegs pa'i nang nas byung ba'o/_/rkang pa g.yas pa'i mthe bong la zin pa dang zhes bya ba ni glo bur gyi rnam par smin pas gzung bas so/_/lus sbrid nas dog sa la 'gyel te 
[\[99a\]]
[\[99a.1\]]zhes bya ba ni/_phyogs rnams mi shes shing /_lus po thams cad skrangs shing 'gas par gyur pa'o/_/dbu bar skyug cing lus kyang bsgyur nas nyal zhing mchis na zhes bya ba ni/_mig 'dzums mi shes shing lus kyang rengs nas kha nas dbugs kyang mi thon pa'o/_/
[\[99a.2\]]btsun pa bcom ldan 'das de la bdag gis ji ltar nan tan bgyi zhes bya ba ni/_sngags dang thabs ci yod ces pa'o/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo'i dge slong sa ri sbrul nag pos zin pa'i gnas skabs te gnyis pa'o//_//de skad ces gsol pa dang 
[\[99a.3\]]zhes bya ba ni/_dge slong sa ri'i don la tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bos smras pa'i rting la'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to zhes bya ba ni mtshan bzang po sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa/_dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu dang ldan pa/_
[\[99a.4\]]stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can gyi don la/_kun dga' bo'i drung du 'khor bzhi dang chas te khyu mchog dang seng ge'i sgra sgrogs pa'o/_/kun dga' bo de bzhin gshegs pa'i bkas khyod song la zhes bya ba ni/_sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[99a.5\]]dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mar me mdzad kyi drung du rjes su yi rang zhing gsungs pa'o/_/de bzhin du yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gtsug tor can la sogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang rjes su yi rang zhing gsungs pa ste/_de bzhin 
[\[99a.6\]]du ma 'ongs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang rjes su yi rang zhing gsungs pa'o/_/da ltar yang ngas kyang gsungs te/_khyod kyis kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' la brten la mdzod cig ces pa'o/_/de bzhin du gshegs pas na/_de bzhin gshegs pa ste/_yang 
[\[99a.7\]]na ngo bo nyid dang bral zhing bde ba chen po thob pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin ni/_/mtshan ma med pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_smon pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi rang bzhin thob pa/_de 'dra'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' la nyon la gsungs shig ces pa'o/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya 
[\[99b\]]
[\[99b.1\]]chen mo 'dis zhes bya ba ni/_rig sngags ni rnam pa gnyis te/_phyi'i rig pa dang /_nang gi rig pa'o/_/phyi'i rig pa ni/_rig pa rnam pa lnga mkhyen cing /_yang dag pa'i ye shes kyi lam nas pha rol tu phyin pa drug spyad nas rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'o/_/nang gi rig pa 
[\[99b.2\]]ni yang dag pa'i ye shes te/_ngo bo nyid dang bral zhing shes rab kyi rang bzhin mi dmigs pa'i ye shes so/_/rgyal mo ni thams cad las rgyal te/_de yang bdud rnams kyis mi tshugs shing 'khor ba bcom pa'o/_/chen mo ni nam mkha' bzhin du khyab ste/_sems can gyi don 
[\[99b.3\]]la thugs rjes khyab pa'o/_/rma bya ni sgyu ma lta bu ste/_sgyu ma'i rang bzhin gyis stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams la sogs pa'i sems can la snying rjes khyab nas rig pa'i lam nas 'jug pa'o/_/yang na rma bya ni sku gsum gyi rang bzhin yin te/_nam mkha' 
[\[99b.4\]]lta bu ni dmigs pa'i rang bzhin chos kyi sku'o/_/de'i grib ma ni sgyu ma lta bur longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'o/_/des sems can la don byed cing nam mkha' lta bur khyab ste sprul pa'i sku'o/_/yang na rma bya ni yang dag pa'i ye shes kyis phyi'i dug dang nang gi dug bcom zhing 
[\[99b.5\]]zas su dug za ba'o/_/'dis ni sdug bsngal thams cad dang bral bar byed pa ste/_gzi brjid che ba dang /_mthu che ba dang /_stobs dang ldan zhing /_ngan song gsum 'joms pa ste/_sems can bde la 'god pa'o/_/'di zhes pa rma bya chen mo'o/_/dge slong sa

[\[99b.7\]]slong sa ri la mche ba'i dug bcom zhing nyams pa thams cad skong ba'o/_/de bzhin du gzhan gyi dug kyang srung bar byed pa'o/_/srung ba gyis shig ces pa ni sems can thams cad dang dge slong sa ri la'o/_/sba ba dang zhes bya ba ni rig sngags 'di'i gzi brjid kyis/_dug dang /_

[\[100a.1\]]'byung po dang /_nad dang /_nyams pa dang /_sdig pa dang /_ngan song gsum na gnas pa sbed pa'o/_/yang na [(]snga [,]sba [)]ba ni/_sdug bsngal thams cad las thar zhing gzhan rnams kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/yongs su bskyab pa dang zhes bya ba ni/_sdug bsngal las bskyab

[\[116a.1\]]pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi bka'o/_/kun dga' bo zhes bya ba ni/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pas sems can gyi don la kun dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/_de'i tshe de'i dus na zhes bya ba ni sngon mi de'i dus na rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo byung 
[\[116a.2\]]ba'i dus so/_/rma bya'i rgyal mo gser du snang ba zhes bya ba de zhes bya ba ni/_'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis gsung zhing /_rjes su yi rang ba sdug bsngal thams cad dang bral bar byed pa/_phyi'i dug dang nang gi dug 'joms pa'o/_/
[\[116a.3\]]gzhan yin pa snyam du khyod sems na/_de ltar mi lta'o zhes bya ba ni/_sngon yang gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma stong phrag brgyad khri brgyad cu rnams de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs te/_de ltar yang ngas gsungs pa'o/_/de bzhin du ma 'ongs pa rnams kyis kyang gsung bar 'gyur ro/_/de

[\[116a.6\]]nyid/_/'gro ba'i gnyug ma bde ba nyid/_/blo dang ldan pas 'bad pa med/_/sgro btags kun las nges grol ba/_/bdag gi de nyid du ni brjod/_/stong pa la sogs mngon brjod pa/_/de la sgro btags brjod pa yis/_/zhes bstan pa'o/_/dbu ma'i lam nas rig pa'i don 
[\[116a.7\]]bstan pa'o/_/de bas na kun dga' bo zhes brjod pa'o/_/nga nyid de'i tshe de'i dus na rma bya'i rgyal mo gser du snang ba zhes bya bar gyur to zhes bya ba ni/_de'i ngo bo nyid rig pa'i rgyal mor snang ste/_de ni kun rdzob kyi rang bzhin gyis sems can gyi don la snang bar gyur pa'o/_/
[\[116b\]]
[\[116b.1\]]kun dga' bo zhes bya ba ni sems can gyi don la bka' stsal pa'o/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo'i snying po 'di yang dag par bzlas par bya'o zhes bya ba ni sems can gyi don gyi glags pa de nyid yin te/_stong pa nyid kyis ma grub pas/_ma man+t+ra'i don du skyabs dang

[\[117a.1\]]bstan pa'o/_/sbrul gyis yan lag brgyan pa ni/_ma rungs pa'i gdug pa can klu dang gnod sbyin la sogs pa 'dul ba'i phyir ro/_/rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa ni/_pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi rnam par dag pa'o/_/phyag brgyad du bstan pa ni/_rnam par

[\[120b\]]
[\[120b.1\]]gzi brjid dang /_stobs dang /_kha dog dang /_grags pa dang ldan te/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la phyag rgya'i rgyas gdab cing sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang rjes su yi rang zhing gsungs pa'o/_/'jig rten 'di'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_dmyal ba'i gnas 
[\[120b.2\]]nas gzugs med pa'i khams rnams so/_/dmyal ba la tsha ba'i dmyal ba dang /_grang ba'i dmyal ba dang gnyis gcig tu brtsis te brgyad do/_/byol song dang [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]gnyis te bcu'o/_/mi yul gling bzhi la so sor bgrangs pas bzhi/_lha la drug go/_/de ni 
[\[120b.3\]]'jig rten 'dod pa'i khams so/_/bsam gtan dang po nas bzung nas/_gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha man chad ni gzugs khams kyi 'jig rten no/_/skye mched mu bzhi gzugs med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams so/_/de bas na 'jig rten 'di'i lha rnams dang zhes pa'o/_/
[\[120b.4\]]bdud rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_ga ra dbang phyug gi 'khor bdud kyi lha rigs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/tshangs par spyod pa zhes bya ba ni/_mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams bdag po tshangs pa nas gzugs khams kyi lha man chad do/_/dge slong dang zhes bya ba ni/_rab tu byung zhing 
[\[120b.5\]]tshangs par spyod pa'i gzugs snang ba'o/_/bram ze'i lus su skyes pa'am zhes bya ba ni/_mi'i 'jig rten gyi khams su rigs bzang por skyes nas gnas pa rnams so/_/skye dgu'i bdag po rnams zhes bya ba ni/_lha dang mi'i bye brag tu smra ba rnams so/_/lha dang mi dang lha ma yin 
[\[120b.6\]]rnams so zhes bya ba ni/_gzhan yang de rnams kyis kyang rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo la gus par byas nas/_de rnams la yang bsrung ba dang /_sba ba dang /_bskyang bar bya bar 'gyur ro/_/sa bcing ba las rnam par 'tshe ba'i phyir 'ong ba zhes bya ba ni/_'byung po de rnams 
[\[120b.7\]]dge ba la bar du gcod cing glags tshol ba rnams yin te/_de ni bdud kyi rang bzhin 'khor ba la gnas par byed pa'i rgyu nad rnams sna tshogs btang ba'i bdag po dang sems can rnams la gnod pa skyel zhing sa dang bar snang sa 'og chu la rgyu ba rnams so/_/de rnams kyi stobs ni ri 
[\[121a\]]
[\[121a.1\]]rab tsam sgul zhing sa g.yo bar byed do/_/de ni gzugs kyi rang bzhin can yin te/_yid kyi rang bzhin can no/_/de rnams kyis sems can rnams la glags tshol zhing 'tshe bar byas pa'o/_/lha'am zhes bya ba ni lha'i 'byung po stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha mo'am zhes 
[\[121a.2\]]bya ba ni lha mo'i gzugs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha'i bu'am zhes bya ba ni lha'i bu stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha'i bu mo'am zhes bya ba ni lha'i bu mo'i gzugs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha rgan po'am zhes bya ba ni/_lha rgan po'i gzugs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha rgan mo'am zhes 
[\[121a.3\]]bya ba ni/_lha rgan mo'i gzugs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha'i 'khor pho'am zhes bya ba ni/_lha'i 'khor pho'i gzugs stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/lha'i 'khor mo nas gnon po'i 'khor mo'am zhes bya ba la sogs pa stong phrag brgyad cu brgyad cu rnams so/_/de rnams kyis glags 
[\[121a.4\]]tshol ba ni ci'i phyir zhe na/_'byung po ma rungs pa rnams gzugs sna tshogs su sprul zhing glags tshol ba'o/_/de dag gi ni mdun rgyug pa/_/'byung po gnod sbyin lus po che/_/lag pa bzhi pa ma rungs pa/_/rkang [{]mangs [,]mang [}]dang ni rkang gcig pa/_/rkang bzhi pa la rkang gnyis pa/_/
[\[121a.5\]]rkang pa bsgreng ba spyi'u tshugs dang /_/lus mang mgo bo gcig pa dang /_/lus gcig pa la mgo bzhi pa/_/mig mang po la lus gcig dang /_/mig gcig lto ni bcu gnyis dang /_/bong bu rnga mo glang chen mgo/_/rkang pa bsgreng la mgo btud dang /_/mtshon cha'i so dang mtshon 
[\[121a.6\]]cha'i lag_/mtshon cha'i rkang pa'i srin po rnams/_/zangs kyi skra dang zangs kyi so/_/zangs kyi mig dang zangs kyi lag_/zangs kyi tho ba'i rkang pa dang /_/zangs kyi sna dang lcags kyi kha/_/rkang lag kun du 'bar ba dang /_/mi yi lus su gnas pa rnams/_/sgur zhar lba 
[\[121a.7\]]ba gsum yod pa/_/mig mi sdug la kha glal ba/_/chu srin stag gi gzugs dang ni/_/gzugs mi sdug dang kha ral bo/_/mchu 'phyangs po dang so yon po/_/rab tu gtum po khro gnyer can/_/gsus po che dang bum sna'o/_/sna 'phyangs po dang rna ba med/_/lag rings po dang 
[\[121b\]]
[\[121b.1\]]rna ba ring /_/sna yi rtse dang lag rtse ring /_/lus skams lus ni ring ba dang /_/skra ring rab tu brgyan pa dang /_/rkang pa mid pa phra ba dang /_/dri nga ba dang bum lto bo/_/yan lag chu srin 'dra ba dang /_/gtun shing tho ba 'dra ba'i lto/_/gnam lto bo la rna bo che/_/gyen du skra brdzes rab tu dmar/_/
[\[121b.2\]]mgo bo che la gzhu mgrin po/_/skem po sgur po bum lto bo/_/ri rab rtse mo bsgul nas su/_/me mdag gi ni char pa 'bebs/_/ljon shing ri yi rdo ba dang /_/sprin gyi tshogs kyi mdog 'dra ba/_/dung dang rnga chen rdza rnga dang /_/grul bum rnga chen rnams kyi sgra/_/'jigs pa'i sgra ni rab tu 'byin/_/
[\[121b.3\]]mig po che la bong bu'i sgra/_/nag po sngon po ser po dang /_/dmar ser me dang dmar ser dang /_/khab 'dra'i spu dang ral gri'i skra/_/dmar po'i khrag gis bskus pa rnams/_/mi ro dag ni khyer nas su/_/za bzhin du ni rnam par rgyug_/so rnon lag pa dmar ba dang /_/mchu ni khrag 
[\[121b.4\]]gis bskus pa dang /_/lus phyed dag ni zos pa dang /_/mkhal ma snying dang nang grol gyis/_/snyim pa shin tu bkang ba dang /_/byis pa dag ni za bzhin du/_/mer mer po dang srog chags kyi/_/rkang lag rnams kyi stobs kyang 'phrog_/keng rus kyi ni lus kyis su/_/skye bo mang po 
[\[121b.5\]]skrag par byed/_/khrag gis rab tu bkang ba yi/_/mi yi pags pa thogs nas su/_/mi bzad dug gis kun bskus te/_/phyogs phyogs dag tu rnam par rgyug_/grong khyer rnams kyi sgo dag dang /_/khyim nas khyim du rnam par 'thor/_/rlung dang bad kan mkhris pa 
[\[121b.6\]]rnams/_/phyogs bzhi dag tu 'khrug par byed/_/de ltar de ni rgyal po yi/_/gdon dang 'jigs pa chen po rnams/_/lhags pa de dag thams cad kyang /_/'byung po de dag rnams kyis kyang /_/glags tshol ba yi dus su yang /_/mi'i 'jig rten 'di la rmi lam dang rtags 
[\[121b.7\]]bstan pa'o/_/phyogs bcu dag tu 'jigs pa skyed/_/bud med dag dang skyes pa dang /_/de bzhin khye'u bu mo dang /_/mngal na gnas pa'ang 'jigs pa skyed/_/dud 'gro'i skye gnas gtogs la'ang 'tshe/_/skar ma dag dang nyi zla dang /_/gtum pa'i gza' rnams gnod par bgyid/_/
[\[122a\]]
[\[122a.1\]]lo tog sa bon 'bras bu dang /_/me tog sman dang zas skom dang /_/mi rnams kyi ni dpal 'phrog cing /_/mthon po rnams kyang dma' bar bgyid/_/'jig rten dag na nongs pa rnams/_/dmag dang 'thab mo sha gnyer dang /_/tshig gam zhig gam gsad bya ba/_/de kun 
[\[122a.2\]]'byung po'i rnam pas gyur/_/sbed cing slar yang bgyid pa dang /_/bzlog par bgyid cing dgod par bgyid/_/'jigs par bgyid cing lta bar bgyid/_/dgrar 'gyur ba yang ston par bgyid/_/sdig pa'i rmi lam mthong bar bgyid/_/nyal ba dag la gnod par bgyid/_/sgo nas se gol sgra 
[\[122a.3\]]'byung zhing /_/mi snyan brjod de za bar bgyid/_/mdza' bo gnyen bshes gzugs dag tu/_/gda' zhing bka' mchid smra bar bgyid/_/bu mo sdug gu'i gzugs dag tu/_/'dod la spyod la dga' bar bgyid/_/nyi ma skar ma'i gzugs dag dang /_/zla ba'i gzugs su gda' bar bgyid/_/
[\[122a.4\]]rlung dang skar mda' lce gzugs su/_/de la gnod pa de dag gda'/_/skar ma'i tshogs dang 'dra ba dag_/'jigs par bgyid pa wa dang khyi/_/rnam par sprul nas ljon shing dang /_/mchod rten dag gi gnas na gda'/_/skyes pa gzhon nu'i gzugs mdzes pas/_/'bar zhing shing rta'i sgra 
[\[122a.5\]]yang 'byin/_/gnas rnams dang ni shul dag tu/_/rnam pa sna tshogs rjes kyang ston/_/grong khyer dag na khyim rnams kyi/_/sgo yang gdon chen de dag 'byed/_/srog dang lus kyang blang bgyis shing /_/lam ngan kun du bskor bar bgyid/_/rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs dag dang /_/rnam 
[\[122a.6\]]pa sna tshogs sgra rnams dang /_/rnam pa sna tshogs nad kyi rnams/_/lus ni na bar gnas par ston/_/nad rnams kun gyi mtshan nyid kyang /_/phyin ci log tu ston par bgyid/_/ji snyed 'jig rten rang bzhin dag_/thams cad log par de dag ston/_/yang na 'byung po de rnams mi'i 'jig rten 
[\[122a.7\]]la gnod par byas nas glags tshol zhing grong ngam grong khyer du rgyu na/_mtshan nyid de bzhin du yin te/_/grong khyer grong rdal yul 'khor dang /_/pho brang 'khor du rgyu ba ni/_/gnod sbyin ma rungs mdangs 'phrog pa/_/snying rje med cing khrag 'thung ba/_/lus che 'jigs pa chen po ste/_/
[\[122b\]]
[\[122b.1\]]stobs po che la sgra yang che/_/mgrin bcu po la mig stong po/_/gdon chen mig ni dmar po rnams/_/'khor ni mang po rnams btang ste/_/lag na mtshon thogs rab tu 'jigs/_/sbrul gyi pags pa thogs pa dang /_/lag bzhi lag na sgron ma thogs/_/lag na mtshon dang 
[\[122b.2\]]dbyug pa thogs/_/rdo rje mdung rtse dbyug pa thogs/_/rab tu drag pa'i dmag rgal nas/_/phyogs kun du ni skrag par byed/_/sdang ba'i gnas na 'khod pa yi/_/gsang ba po ni dad kun kyang /_/mi yi 'jig rten 'di dag tu/_/skyes pa bud med gzhan yang 'dzin/_/sha 
[\[122b.3\]]khrag dron por bcas pa la/_/rnam par 'phrul zhing 'dod pa'i gzugs/_/seng ge dang ni stag dang rta/_/ma he ba lang bong bu rnga/_/glang po che yi gzugs 'dra dang /_/dom dang gzig dang spyang ki dang /_/khyi dang wa dang ra dag dang /_/byi la byi ba [(]sbre'u [,]spre'u [)]yi gzugs/_/bse dang phag 
[\[122b.4\]]dang sre mo dang /_/nya dang rus sbal dag gi gzugs/_/kha cig dag ni sram gyi gzugs/_/khwa dang 'ug pa khu byug dang /_/bya rgod khra dang nya mid bcas/_/rma bya ngang pa thi ba dang /_/khrung khrung dang ni bya rnams dang /_/kha cig bya gag gzugs rnams su/_/gnod sbyin rnams ni mi 
[\[122b.5\]]yis mthong /_/'dab [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]bya yi tshul gyis kyang /_/skye bo mang po skrag par byed/_/kha cig mi yi mgo can la/_/lus ni bya gag bong bu 'dra/_/kha cig mtshon gyis rab tu 'tshe/_/lus dang bral ba dag tu snang /_/rnam par 'khrugs shing 'dod la mos/_/rgyu ma'i phreng 
[\[122b.6\]]bas kun du dkris/_/rtse gsum mdung gis rab 'debs shing /_/srog chags rnams la gnod par byed/_/mi bzad pa yi dbugs 'byin zhing /_/skye dgu 'di dag skrag par byed/_/srog chags rgyud ni ji snyed pa/_/sna tshogs kyi ni gzugs su snang /_/kha cig dag ni ri yang 
[\[122b.7\]]thogs/_/la la ral gri 'khor lo thogs/_/grangs ni brgya phrag stong gnas te/_/dbyug pas bsdigs shing kha drag po/_/mig phyung ba la bzhin mi sdug_/srin po'i gzugs ni sngo ba dang /_/sna dang rna ba bcad pa dang /_/lce bcad gdong ni gnyer ma can/_/rkang lag 
[\[123a\]]
[\[123a.1\]]dag ni gtubs pa dang /_/mgo bo bcad pa'i srin po rnams/_/skabs ni rab tu tshol bar byed/_/sdig pa can rnams gzi brjid 'phrog_/mi dag gi ni lus rnams la/_/rab tu phra ba'i gdon du snang /_/spu yi bar dang gnad rnams dang /_/de bzhin rma yi kha rnams su/_/
[\[123a.2\]]lhags pa de dag thams cad kyang /_/de bzhin du lha pho mo dang /_klu pho mo dang gnod sbyin pho mo la sogs pas skad cig thang cig la/_sems can la 'tshe bar byed pa nad sna tshogs gtong zhing 'byung po rnams kyis kyang glags tshol ba'o/_/glags tshol bar 'dod cing glags lta na 
[\[123a.3\]]yang glags mi rnyed de zhes bya ba ni/_lha'i gdon dang bcas pa'i 'byung po rnams kyis kyang gnod par byed mi nus shing /_rtsar yang 'ong mi nus kyi/_glags tshol ba lta ci smos so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo 'di/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
[\[123a.4\]]sangs rgyas rnams kyis/_rjes su yi rang zhing gsungs pa/_/gzi brjid che ba stobs dang ldan pa/_'byung po rnams kyi gnyen por gsungs pa'o/_/de bas na glags tshol bas mi rnyed pa'o/_/lha ni lha 'du ba'i gnas su ma yin zhes bya ba la sogs pa nas/_gnon po ni gnon po 
[\[123a.5\]]'du ba'i gnas su ma yin no zhes bya ba ni/_rig sngags kyi gzi brjid kyis 'du bar mi nus shing 'byung po rnams kyang so sor 'khyams te/_gnod par mi nus shing /_rtsar nye bar mi nus pa'o/_/gang la la zhig rig sngags 'di las 'gal bar byed na zhes bya ba ni/_gal te 
[\[123a.6\]]glags tshol ba rnams rig sngags kyi bka' las 'das par gyur na/_sdug bsngal dpag tu med pa 'thob ste/_dmyal ba mnar med du skye bar 'gyur ro/_/dmyal ba de las thon nas yang nas yang du gzhan du dmyal bar 'pho'o/_/de'i mgo bo ardza ka'i dog pa bzhin du 
[\[123a.7\]]tshal pa bdun du 'gas so zhes bya ba ni/_rig sngags kyi dmod mos mdze'am phol mig gam/_lhog pa'am 'bras la sogs pa rnams kyis btab nas/_shi ba'i dus su tshal pa bdun du 'gas so/_/gsang sngags kyi gzhi dag kyang yid la bya'o zhes bya ba ni 'byung po rnams 
[\[123b\]]
[\[123b.1\]]kyi gnyen po ni/_rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i gsang sngags kyi thabs bshad par bya'o/_/slob dpon las kyi rdo rjes tshigs su bcad pa 'di gsungs so/_/dang por dgug pa/_gnyis pa bskrad pa/_gsum pa mnan pa/_bzhi pa bsreg pa'o/_/
[\[123b.2\]]gang gnod pa'i 'byung po rnams/_rig sngags kyi nga rgyal byas nas/_phyag g.yas g.yon gyis dgug pa'i phyag rgya bya ste/_rdo rje khu tshur yang dag par bcas la/_mdzub mo gnyis kyis dgug pa'i tshul du'o/_/rig sngags kyi gsang sngags la/_oM badzra aM ku sha dzaHla rig 
[\[123b.3\]]sngags kyi gsang sngags btags la dgug par bya'o/_/ma rungs pa'i 'byung po rnams/_oM dang rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi/_/rig sngags smon gyis 'dug mi nus/_/ri rab dur khrod pho brang dang /_/rgya mtsho lung stong shing gcig dang /_/nags tshal dag dang rgya gram dang /_/ri yi 
[\[123b.4\]]rtse mo mchod rten rtsa/_/khron pa dag dang chu mig dang /_/mtsho chen dang ni tshal gnas dang /_/de bzhin grong khyer grong gnas dang /_/grong rdal dang ni pho brang dang /_/ri yi rtse mo nam mkha' dang /_/bar snang chu dang sa la gnas/_/de bzhin sa 'og lam la gnas/_/dzaHhUM baM hoH'i 
[\[123b.5\]]phyag rgya yis/_/'byung po mang po rig sngags dgug_/'byung po ma rungs pa rnams rig sngags kyi phyag rgyas dgug pa'o/_/yang bskrad pa ni/_yungs kar ske tshe dang bsres la/_ra bar tsher ma can gyi shing bu ste/_mdzod dang khyim gyi phyogs mtshams kyi sa blangs la ske tshe dang 
[\[123b.6\]]yungs kar bsres la yang bden pa'i tshigs bcad bton la/_bde gshegs rnams kyi gzi brjid kyis/_/gtsug tor can gyi stobs rnams dang /_/thams cad skyob pa'i bden tshig dang /_/log dad sel gyi ting 'dzin dang /_/gser thub kyi ni ye shes kyis/_/'od srungs kyi ni 
[\[123b.7\]]rdzu 'phrul lo/_/shAkya seng ge'i brtson 'grus so/_/'byung po de dag brlag par shog_/u tsa ta ya phaT rnams kyis/_/rig sngags snying po de btags la/_/rnga bo che dang 'khar rnga dang /_/de la sogs pa sgra yi tshogs/_/de brdungs 'byung po bskrad par bya/_/me tog sna tshogs 
[\[124a\]]
[\[124a.1\]]gtor ma dang /_/rtsa ba sna tshogs lo ma dang /_/'bru sna chu dang bcas pa dang /_/'ongs pa'i phyogs su bskyal bar bya/_/de bzhin bskrad nas mnan par bya'o/_/gro ga la sogs shing lo la/_/'byung po'i gzugs dang 'khor lo bcas/_/de yi nang du ming byang bri/_/yungs 
[\[124a.2\]]kar lan tshwa ske tshe dang /_/shang tshe dug rdzas ro thang dang /_/de rnams bcug la dril bar bya/_/rabs chad mdze thod nang du gzhug_/sngar bshad dgug gzhug 'byung po la/_/rig sngags la ni stam+b+ha ya gdags/_/lam mam rgya gram mchod rten 'og_/pho khrom sar ni mnan par bya/_/de bzhin 
[\[124a.3\]]mnan la bsreg par bya/_/tsan+dan la sogs dri rnams kyi/_/de yi chus ni lus rnams bkru/_/smyung bar byas nas gos gtsang gyon/_/seng ldeng shing la zhugs phul la/_/'bru dang rtsa ba sna tshogs dang /_/gnod byed gang la ming btags la/_/zhugs kyi nang du gtor bar bgyi/_/
[\[124a.4\]]gsang sngags la ni me sngags gdags/_/'byung po'i rnam pa ma lus bsreg_/gtor ma me tog lo ma dang /_/chos skyong la ni sbyin par bya/_/de nas bden pa'i tshig bshad 'di/_/yang dag gis ni brjod par bya/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi gzi brjid kyis/_/rang rgyal dag gi gzi brjid dang /_/
[\[124a.5\]]dgra bcom pa yi mthu dag gis/_/gsang sngags 'dzin pa thams cad dang /_/shA ri'i bu yi shes rab dang /_/maud gal bu yi rdzu 'phrul dang /_/ma 'gags pa yi mig dang ni/_/'od srungs sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang /_/kauN+Di n+ya'i sngar thos dang /_/kun dga' bo yi thos pa 
[\[124a.6\]]dang /_/tshangs pa yi ni byams pa dang /_/brgya byin gyi ni dbang rnams dang /_/'jig rten skyong ba'i yul rnams thob/_/dbang phyug chen po'i stobs dag dang /_/ded dpon rnams kyi mthu dag gis/_/'phrog ma'i gzi brjid rdzu 'phrul ldan/_/de dag rnams kyi mthu dag gis/_/
[\[124a.7\]]bdag gi gnod byed bsreg par shog_/'byung po dug dang dgra bo dang /_/bdag gi 'jigs pa thams cad dang /_/sangs rgyas bden pa de rnams kyis/_/sems can dag dang bdag rnams dang /_/sdig pa thams cad bsreg par shog_/gsang sngags tshig rnams bden 
[\[124b\]]
[\[124b.1\]]par shog_/de bzhin du yang nas yang du gsang sngags la me'i sngags btags la bsreg par bgyi'o/_/de nas rig sngags ni gang zhe na kun gzhi rnam par dag pa ste/_thabs dang shes rab gnyis su med pa'o/_/mtshon pa ni me long lta bu'i ye shes so/_/mtshon byed ni 
[\[124b.2\]]rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo'o/_/rdo rje'i rigs so/_/rnam par shes pa'i phung po'o/_/nam mkha'i khams so/_/yid kyi skye mched do/_/zhe sdang gi rang bzhin no/_/sangs rgyas rigs lnga'i dbu rgyan no/_/spyan gsum pa zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa/_khro gnyer 
[\[124b.3\]]can kha gdangs pa/_mche ba gtsigs pa/_'jigs su rung ba/_sku mdog nag mo/_/yan lag thams cad rin po che dang sbrul gyis brgyan pa/_sham thabs stag gis byas pa/_g.yas dang po na rdo rje rtse lnga gdengs pa/_de'i 'og ma na ni ral gri/_g.yon dang po na sdigs mdzub 
[\[124b.4\]]dang zhags pa/_de'i 'og ma na lcags kyu/_zhabs g.yas bskum pa g.yon brkyang ba gnyis na ma rungs pa'i 'byung po thams cad mnan pa'o/_/nyi ma'i gdan la me yi 'od/_skra ser po gyen du'o/_/me ltar 'bar ba rigs lnga'i dbu rgyan de ni thod pas brgyan pa'o/_/
[\[124b.5\]]dbu'i spyi bo'i gtsug na rma bya'i sgro lngas brgyan pa/_de ni ye shes lnga'i rang bzhin no/_/pad+ma'i gdan la bzhugs shing khro mor bsam mo/_/de bzhin du rig sngags kyi gsang sngags yid la bya'o/_/rig sngags kyi gsang sngags kyi tshig ni gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_i li mi li 
[\[124b.6\]]ki li la sogs pa'o/_/gsang sngags 'di'i tshig la 'gal na/_shi ba'i dus su mgo bo tshal par 'gas shing /_shi ma thag mnar med par 'gro ba'o/_/bdag la bsrung ba gyis shig ces bya ba ni gang zag sems can rnams la'o/_/sba ba dang yongs su bskyab pa dang zhes bya 
[\[124b.7\]]ba ni/_khams gsum na rgyu ba'i 'byung po thams cad sba ba'o/_/de bzhin du bskyab pa yang yin no/_/yongs su gzung ba dang zhes bya ba ni/_dper na rang gi nor gzhan du mi ster ba bzhin no/_/'byung po de rnams la yang mi ster ba'o/_/yongs su bskyang ba dang zhes bya ba ni/_rig 
[\[125a\]]
[\[125a.1\]]sngags kyi rgyal mo'i gzi brjid kyis/_glang chen smyon pa bzhin du thub pa med pa'o/_/zhi ba dang zhes bya ba ni 'byung po thams cad dang nad zhi bar byed pa'o/_/bde legs su 'gyur ba dang zhes bya ba ni/_bkra shis shing nad rnams bcom pa'o/_/chad pa spang ba dang

[\[198b.2\]]ngos nas med pa 'og tu 'dzul te/_des na chu chen po nai rany+dza na zhes bya'o/_/mtsho chen po bco lnga po de ni srid pa gsum la gnas pa'o/_/ri'i rgyal po spos ngad ldan nas/_de nas dpag tshad bcu'i tshu rol na mtsho chen chu zhes bya ba yod de/_ma dros pa zhes kyang bya'o/_/mtshon chen de'i tshad ni/_chur dpag 
[\[198b.3\]]tshad lnga bcu zheng du dpag tshad lnga bcu/_mthar skor du dpag tshad nyis brgya/_'phang du dpag tshad phyed dang gnyis yod pa/_dbyibs legs shing blta na sdug la/_mdzes pa/_chu bsil ba/_sbrang rtsi lta bus gang ba/_mtsho de'i nang du me tog pad+ma dang /_puN+Da rI ka dang /_ku mu ta dang /_ut+pa la sngon 
[\[198b.4\]]po la sogs pa/_chu las skyes pa'i me tog sna tshogs yod pa'o/_/chu de'i nang du yang bya sna tshogs yod de/_khrung khrung dang /_ngang skya dang /_chu bya nag po dang /_chu bya rke [{]rings [,]ring [}]dang /_ngang ser dang /_ngang skya dang /_ngang skya'i rgyal po dang /_de la sogs pa chu bya sna tshogs pa gnas pa'o/_/
[\[198b.5\]]mtsho chen po ma dros pa bzhin du gzhan yang de bzhin du gnas pa'o/_/de dag dang gzhan yang gang dag sa'i dkyil 'khor 'dir 'bab pa'i chu klung de dag thams cad la zhes bya ba ni/_srid pa gsum na gnas pa'i chu klung gi rgyal po bzhi bcu rtsa gcig la gnas pa'o/_/gang dag rgyun du gnas 
[\[198b.6\]]pa zhes bya ba ni/_chu la gnas pa/_lha dang lha ma yin/_gnod sbyin 'byung po ma rungs pa la sogs pa'o/_/gzugs tha dad pa zhes bya ba ni/_rkang [{]mangs [,]mang [}]dang /_rkang gnyis rkang bzhi rkang drug rkang brgyad la sogs pa'o/_/gzugs mi sdug pa zhes bya ba ni bzhin mi sdug cing /_sbrul dang /_sbal 
[\[198b.7\]]pa dang /_nya dang /_bya dang /_khyi dang /_wa dang /_spyang ki dang /_chu srin dang /_kum+b+hi ra la sogs pa'i mgo brnyan no/_/gzugs mang ba zhes bya ba ni/_sems can la la chu srin gyi gzugs su yod pa/_rta'i gzugs su yod pa/_phag gi gzugs su yod pa/_glang po che'i gzugs su yod pa/_sbrul gyi 
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]gzugs la sogs pa/_sems can gzugs mi gcig pa mang po yod pa'o/_/gzugs mtha' yas pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_chung ba dang /_che ba dang /_bsam gyis mi khyab pa yod pa'o/_/'dod dgur sgyur ba'i gzugs can gzugs sna tshogs pa'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_srid 
[\[199a.2\]]pa gsum na gnas pa'i lha la sogs pa'i 'byung po stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhi'o/_/zas su gzi brjid 'phrog pa rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_zas su gzi brjid 'phrog pa nas/_rigs mi gcig pa sum cu rtsa gcig_/srid pa gsum na gnas pa'i chu klung chen po gnas pa'o/_/de yang 
[\[199a.3\]]gzugs mi gcig pa mang por gnas pa/_srid gsum skad cig rgyu ba'o/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo 'dis bdag la srung ba gyis shig ces bya ba ni/_gang zag gang gis rig sngags kyi rgyal mo 'di la gus par byas par gyur na/_chu klung chen po de rnams la yod pa rnams kyis 
[\[199a.4\]]kyang yang dag par srung ba dang skyob par 'gyur ro/_/sbra ba la sogs pa dang zhes bya ba ni/_'byung po de rnams kyis kyang snga ma bshad pa bzhin du byas pa'o/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal mo rma bya chen mo las chu klung bshad pa'i gnas skabs te bcu drug pa'o//_//kun dga' bo zhes bya ba ni/_yang dag par 
[\[199a.5\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pas gsungs pa'o/_/khyod kyis ri'i rgyal po rnams kyi ming zung shig ces bya ba ni/_srid pa chags pa'i dus su chags pa/_ri rab la sogs pa ri'i rgyal po chen po lnga bcu rtsa bzhi'i ming zung shig ces pa'o/_/'di lta ste zhes bya ba ni ri'i rgyal po rnams 
[\[199a.6\]]kyi ming blta ba'o/_/ri'i rgyal po ri rab dang zhes bya ba ni/_dpag tshad brgyad khri ni thur du gser gyi sa gzhi la brten to/_/dpag tshad brgyad khri ni chu las gyen du 'phags pa'o/_/'phang du dpag tshad 'bum drug khri yod do/_/rngams su dpag tshad brgyad khri/_zheng du dpag tshad brgyad khri yod pa/_

[\[199b.4\]]ri rab ni/_bang rim bzhi yod de/_bang rim dang po ni chur las dpag tshad khris gyen du 'phags pa la ri'i rgyal po ri rab las dpag tshad khri drug stong phyir 'phags te/_dbyibs legs shing blta na sdug la mdzes pa/_rin po che sna bzhi las grub pa/_de ni lag na yol go thogs kyi lha rnams so/_/

[\[199b.7\]]las/_dpag tshad khri gyen du 'phags la/_ri'i rgyal po ri rab las dpag tshad nyis stong phyir 'phags te/_dbyibs legs shing blta na sdug la mdzes shing /_rin po che sna bzhi las grub pa/_de'i phyogs bzhi na rgyal po bzhi rnams gnas so/_/ri'i rgyal po gangs can

[\[200a.1\]]dang zhes bya ba ni/_sgra mi snyan nas ri nag po dgu 'das pa'i byang phyogs na ri'i rgyal po gangs zhes bya ba yod de/_/ri'i rgyal po spos kyi ngad ldang dang zhes bya ba ni/_ri nag po dgu 'das pa'i pha rol na yod do/_/ri'i rgyal po rtse brgya pa dang zhes bya ba ni/_phyi'i rgya mtsho'i

[\[201a.1\]]grong khyer de'i dbus na lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyi khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rnam par rgyal byed ces bya ba/_chur dpag tshad nyis brgya lnga bcu/_rgyar yang de dang 'dra ste/_mtha' skor du stong yod pa/_lha'i grong khyer las dpag tshad phyed dang lnga gyen du 'phags so/_/rmang rten du yang rin po che sna bzhi

[\[202a.1\]]dpag tshad lnga brgya na shel las grub pa'i ri yod de/_mtha' skor du dpag tshad khri yod pa/_'phang du dpag tshad lnga stong ngo /_/zla ba bzhin du bsil bas na zla 'od ces bya'o/_/ri'i rgyal po brag [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang zhes bya ba ni/_rgya gar gyi nub byang

[\[202a.7\]]ngos na ri bo rtse lnga pa mtha' skor du dpag tshad lnga brgya pa/_'phang du dpag tshad phyed dang sum brgya/_dkar po dang /_ser po dang /_dmar po dang /_sngon po dang /_kha dog mdzes shing snang bas na sna tshogs mdzes zhes bya'o/_/ri'i rgyal po ma la ya dang zhes bya ba ni/_zangs gling gi lho ngos na tsan+dan gyi 
[\[202b\]]
[\[202b.1\]]nags gseb yod de/_mtha' skor du dpag tshad brgya/_'phang du dpag tshad brgya'o/_/de'i lho phyogs na bya khyung yod pa ste/_shin tu drang ba'i phyir na ma la yar btags so/_/ri'i rgyal po gser gyi rtse mo dang zhes bya ba ni/_gshol mda' 'dzin zhes bya ba'o/_/ri'i rgyal po gnya' shing 'dzin

[\[220a.2\]]tshe tshad kyi mtha' ni/_lo de lta bur brtsis pa'i lo nyis stong thub po/_/mi lo ltar brtsis pa'i bye ba phrag bcu bzhi dang /_sa ya bzhi yod do/_/nyon mongs pa ni 'khyud pa tsam gyis zhi ba'o/_/dga' ldan gyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus kyi tshad ni rgyang grags gcig go/_/tshe 
[\[220a.3\]]tshad ni mi lo bzhi brgya la/_dga' ldan gyi lha rnams kyi zhag gcig go/_/de sum cu la zla ba gcig go/_/zla ba bcu gnyis la lo gcig go/_/dga' ldan gyi lha rnams kyi tshe tshad kyi mtha' ni/_de ltar brtsis pa'i lo bzhi stong thub po/_/mi lo ltar brtsis na bye ba phrag 
[\[220a.4\]]lnga bcu rtsa bdun dang /_lo sa ya phrag drug yod do/_/nyon mongs pa ni lag pa nas bzung ba tsam gyis tshim mo/_/'phrul dga'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni rgyang grags gcig dang /_de'i bzhi cha gcig go/_/tshe tshad ni mi lo brgyad brgya la/_'phrul 
[\[220a.5\]]dga'i nyin zhag gcig_/nyin zhag sum cu la zla ba gcig_/zla ba bcu gnyis la lo gcig_/de ltar brtsis pa'i 'phrul dga'i lha rnams kyi tshe tshad kyi mtha' ni/_lha'i lo brgya stong thub/_mi lo ltar brtsis na/_bye ba phrag nyis brgya sum cu dang /_lo sa ya phrag 
[\[220a.6\]]bzhi thub po/_/nyon mongs pa ni bgad pa tsam gyis tshim mo/_/gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni rgyang grags phyed dang gnyis/_tshe tshad ni mi rnams kyi lo stong drug brgya la/_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams kyi zhag gcig 
[\[220a.7\]]go/_/nyin zhag sum cu la zla ba gcig_/zla ba bcu gnyis la lo gcig_/gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi tshe'i mtha' ni/_lo de ltar brtsis pa'i lha'i lo khri drug stong thub/_nyon mongs pa ni bltas pa tsam gyis tshim mo/_/mi lo ltar brtsis pa na/_bye ba phrag

[\[220b.1\]]dgu brgya nyi shu rtsa gcig dang /_lo sa ya phrag drug yod do/_/tshangs ris kyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus kyi tshad ni dpag tshad phyed do/_tshe tshad ni bskal ba phyed do/_/tshangs ris nas 'og min gyi bar du 'dod chags med do/_/tshangs pa mdun na 'don gyi 
[\[220b.2\]]lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus kyi tshad ni dpag tshad gcig_/tshe'i tshad ni bskal pa gcig go/_/'di ni bsam gtan dang po'i lha ste/_tshangs pa mdun na 'don gyi lha'i nges tshig ni/_gong ma'i tshangs pa chen po'i spyan sngar 'og ma'i las dge sdig snang 'don 
[\[220b.3\]]pas na/_tshangs pa mdun na 'don zhes bya'o/_/yang na tshangs pa chen po la phan 'dogs pas/_tshangs pa nye phan zhes bya/_yang na tshangs pa chen po la 'khor bas tshangs pa kun 'khor zhes bya'o/_/tshangs pa chen po'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad 
[\[220b.4\]]gnyis/_tshe tshad ni bskal pa phyed dang gnyis yod de/_tshangs pa chen po'i nges tshig ni/_'dod pa'i khams kyi 'khor ba gyod drin las tshangs pas tshangs pa chen po'o/_/'od chung gi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad gnyis yod do/_/tshe tshad ni bskal 
[\[220b.5\]]pa gnyis thob po/_/nges tshig ni gong ma gnyis pas 'od chung bas so/_/tshad med 'od kyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad bzhi/_tshe tshad ni bskal pa bzhi/_nges tshig ni 'og ma la ltos nas lus dang gzhal med khang tshad med pa'i phyir ro/_/
[\[220b.6\]]tshad ni dpag tshad gnyis yod do/_/'od gsal gyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad brgyad do/_/tshe tshad ni bskal pa brgyad thub/_nges tshig ni 'og ma gzhan gyi 'od kyang /_'di'i 'od kyis gsal bar byed pa'o/_/dge chung gi lha rnams dang

[\[221a.1\]]gnyis pas chung bas so/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi zas la spyod do/_/tshad med dge ba'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad dpag tshad sum cu rtsa gnyis/_tshe tshad bskal pa sum cu rtsa gnyis/_nges tshig ni 'og ma pas gong ma'i rtags tshad mi spobs pas ni

[\[221a.6\]]bsgoms pas/_'bras bu mthar phyin pa'o/_/'du shes med pa'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus kyi tshad ni gong ma dang 'dra'o/_/tshe tshad ni bskal pa lnga brgya'o/_/nges tshig ni 'du shes de shes pa nad du 'khyil bas ngan par shes pa skyes te/_da dung skye ba mang po 
[\[221a.7\]]len pa mthong bas/_khos thar pa mya ngan las 'das pa gtan med pa 'dra snyam nas skur pa btab pas/_dmyal bar ltung ba yod do/_/kha cig blo rno bas 'phar te 'gro ba yang yod/_bsam gtan bsgoms pa nor ba yin zer lus 'od che la dran pa mi rgyu/_mdor na 'du 
[\[221b\]]
[\[221b.1\]]shes med pa'i lha/_gzugs su rags la tshor ba zhan pa'o/_/mi che ba'i lha rnams zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad stong /_tshe tshad kyang bskal pa bzhi stong ngo /_/nges tshig ni rtog pa gong ma tsam du mi che'o/_/mi gdung ba'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad 
[\[221b.2\]]kyang dpag tshad nyis stong /_tshe tshad kyang bskal pa nyis stong /_nges tshig ni khams gsum gyi gdung ba dang bral ba'i phyir ro/_/gya nom snang ba'i lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_lus tshad ni dpag tshad bzhi stong /_tshe tshad kyang bskal pa bzhi stong thub po/_/nges tshig

[\[221b.7\]]dang zhes bya ba ni/_tshe tshad ni bskal pa bzhi khri thub/_nges tshig ni gzugs khams kyi ting nge 'dzin la bltas nas/_chos thams cad nam mkha' ltar mtha' yas so snyam pa ni/_rnam par shes pa yin la/_de yang ma zad de/_rnam par shes pa de dang

[\[222a.1\]]'grogs pa'i 'du shes kyang /_nam mkha' ltar kun du mtha' yas par brtags nas/_skye mched nye bar blangs pa'i phyir ro/_/yod min med min skye mched kyi lha rnams dang zhes bya ba ni/_tshe tshad bskal pa brgyad khri nges tshig ni 'og ma ltar
